| Term | Description |

| [True DMG] | Not affected by DMG Rate or DMG Free, and deals Direct DMG without [DMG Sharing] or [Evasion]. |

| [Charge] | A damage type reduced by [Injury Reduction]. |

| 5 | Passive | **Extreme Passive** | DoT increase 50% → 65%. Recover [Specialized Guard] 40% → 50%. |
| 6 | Passive | **5★ Passive** | DoT increase 65% → 80%. Recover [Specialized Guard] 50% → 60%. |
| 7 | Awakening | **Awaken 1 Passive** | At the start of the battle, all enemies will be inflicted with [Specialized Burn] (This effect is unaffected by Hit, deals continuous damage for 150% ATK that will disappear after 3 times. After any enemy takes action, it will spread to others that does not have this effect). Before any character action, clear [Stun] or [Specialized Stun] of 1 ally up to 2 times per round. | 8 | Awakening | **Awaken 2 Passive** | At the start of battle > For the first 3 rounds Dispel stun 2x per round > 3x Increase allies atk 10% > 15% Self gain permanent immunity to Stun/S.Stun |
| 9 | Awakening | **Awaken 3 Passive** | • Restores 150 [Energy Gauge] (when attacking) |

**Effect Glossary:**

| Term | Description |
|---|---|
| [Spec. Stun] | PvP tier of Stun — harder to resist or resolve. |
| [Stun] | The stun-type control effect prevents the target from taking action during the duration. |
| [Spec. Guard] | PvP-tier shields absorb incoming damage, granted at a value based on Attack (e.g. 400% ATK). |
| [Detonate] | Activate a stack of damage over time or stored [Collapse] to instantly explode, turning it into an instant multiplier (e.g. 3 times the stored damage). |
| [Spec. Burn] | Burning PvP tier — stronger burst damage, harder to deal with. |

| Term | Description |

| [Energy Removal] | An effect that removes Energy. Gale Wind is immune to this effect. |

**Effect Glossary:**

| Term | Description |
|---|---|
| [Special Breakthrough] | Immediately removes the target’s [Specialized Unyielding]. |
| [Specialized Unyielding] | A specialized survival effect that prevents the affected unit from being defeated under specific conditions. |
| [True DMG] | Not affected by DMG Rate or DMG Free, and deals Direct DMG without [DMG Sharing] or [Evasion]. |
| [True DMG Bonus] | Increases the amount of [True DMG] dealt. |
| [DMG Sharing] | Distributes incoming DMG between multiple affected units. [True DMG] ignores this effect. |
| [Evasion] | Allows the affected unit to avoid applicable DMG. [True DMG] ignores this effect. |
| [Special Evasion] | Direct DMG, DoT DMG, Received DMG, and Bonus DMG only deal 1 point of DMG per instance. |
| [Fear] | A control effect that this character is immune to. |
| [Stun] | Prevents the affected unit from acting. |
| [Special Stun] | A specialized form of [Stun]. |
| [Non-Crit Undying] | Prevents fatal non-critical DMG from defeating the character and blocks a limited number of fatal critical-hit instances. |
| [CRIT Resistance] | Reduces the chance of receiving a critical hit. |
| [Non-Crit DMG Free] | Reduces all types of non-critical DMG and cannot coexist with [Agility]. |

**Effect Glossary:**

| Term | Description |
|---|---|
| [Lock] | Prevents [Follow-Up], [Continuous Follow-Up], and [Combo Follow-Up]. Before any character acts, deals ATK-based DoT that can CRIT and cannot be [Evaded]. |
| [Follow-Up] | An additional attack triggered after another action or when a specified condition is met. |
| [Continuous Follow-Up] | A Follow-Up that can continue triggering under specified conditions. |
| [Combo Follow-Up] | A coordinated Follow-Up attack performed under specified conditions. |
| [Evaded] | Causes applicable attacks or DMG effects to miss. [Lock] DoT cannot be evaded. |
| [Non-Crit DMG Free] | Reduces all types of non-critical DMG and cannot coexist with [Agility]. |
| [Agility] | A defensive effect that cannot coexist with [Non-Crit DMG Free]. Exact effect details are currently unknown. |
| [Continuous Explosion] | When an enemy triggers [Lock], spreads a percentage of the [Lock] DMG and DoT DMG to surrounding targets or all enemies. This DMG can CRIT and cannot be dodged. |

**Effect Glossary:**

| Term | Description |
|---|---|
| [Break] | Decreases the affected target’s DMG Rate%. |
| [Specialized Berserk] | Causes the first strike to deal [Berserk DMG] based on King’s Max HP. The DMG can CRIT and consumes Energy Gauge for each enemy hit. |
| [Berserk DMG] | A special damage type dealt by the first strike while [Specialized Berserk] is active. |
| [Specialized Guard] | A defensive effect whose Max HP value contributes additional DMG to the Ultra-Ultimate’s [Specialized Berserk]. |
| [Overflow Recovery] | Converts excess Healing and Drain into an equal amount of [Life Shield]. The accumulated DMG removed cannot exceed 20× the unit’s original HP. |
| [Life Shield] | A shield generated from excess Healing and Drain through [Overflow Recovery]. |
| [Paralysis] | A negative control effect removed from King and allies in the same row by Awaken 2 Passive. |
| [Combo Follow-Up] | Performs the Ultimate as a follow-up against random targets with a specified DMG percentage and 100% of its effects. It is not affected by Hit Rate or Effect Resistance. |
| [Shared DMG] | Direct DMG transferred from allies in the same row to King. The transferred DMG is reduced before King receives it. |

**Effect Glossary:**
| Term | Description |
|---|---|
| [Specialized Extra DMG] | Additional damage that can CRIT and is unaffected by [Specialized DMG Reflect]. |
| [Specialized DMG Reflect] | A specialized effect that reflects applicable damage. It does not affect Metal Bat’s [Specialized Extra DMG]. |
| [Non-Crit DMG Free] | Reduces all types of non-critical DMG and cannot coexist with [Agility]. |
| [Agility] | A defensive effect that cannot coexist with [Non-Crit DMG Free]. Exact effect details are currently unknown. |
| [HP Shield] | A shield based on HP that interacts with Metal Bat’s [Momentum]. |
| [Momentum] | An enhancement gained based on the highest number of Metal Bat’s [Excess Recovery] layers. Each layer increases the Ultimate’s [Specialized Extra DMG]. |
| [Excess Recovery] | A recovery-related effect whose accumulated layers determine the number of [Momentum] layers gained. |

**Effect Glossary:**

| Term | Description |
|---|---|
| [Instant DMG Hit Rate] | Increases the accuracy of Instant DMG effects. |
| [Specialized Hit Rate] | Increases the accuracy of specialized effects or attacks. |
| [Evasion] | Allows the affected unit to avoid applicable DMG. |
| [Specialized Evasion] | A specialized defensive effect that allows the unit to avoid applicable DMG. |
| [Non-Crit DMG Reduction] | Reduces DMG received from non-critical attacks. |
| [Berserk] | An offensive state that grants the specified amount of ATK. |
| [Specialized Breakthrough] | A specialized breakthrough effect that can be triggered once by each hit. Exact effect details are currently unknown. |
| [Regroup] | Allows a defeated unit to return to the battlefield a limited number of times. |
| [Revenge] | Allows a defeated unit to return to battle and perform a pursuit attack under specified conditions. |
| [Specialized Counter DMG] | A specialized counter-damage effect that is dispelled by this Passive. |
| [Fear] | A control effect that this character and allies in the same column become immune to. |
| [Stun] | Prevents the affected unit from acting. |
| [Specialized Stun] | A specialized form of [Stun]. |
| [Momentum Return] | When all allies are defeated, returns the unit to the battlefield with restored HP. Excess recovery is converted into additional Max HP. |
| [Suppression DMG Reduction] | Reduces DMG taken. The reduction is increased when the unit’s ATK is higher than the target’s. |

**Effect Glossary:**

| Term | Description |
|---|---|
| [Lock] | Prevents [Follow-Up], [Continuous Follow-Up], and [Combo Follow-Up]. Before any character acts, deals ATK-based DoT that can CRIT and cannot be [Evaded]. |
| [Follow-Up] | An additional attack triggered after another action or when a specified condition is met. |

| [True DMG] | Not affected by DMG Rate or DMG Free and deals Direct DMG without [DMG Sharing] or [Evasion]. |
| [DMG Sharing] | Distributes incoming DMG among multiple affected units. [True DMG] ignores this effect. |
| [Evasion] | Allows an affected unit to avoid applicable DMG. [True DMG] ignores this effect. |
| [Evaded] | Causes an applicable attack or DMG effect to miss. [Lock] DoT cannot be evaded. |

| Term | Description |
|---|---|

| [Agility] | A defensive effect that cannot coexist with [Non-Crit DMG Free]. |

### Additional Notes

- SSR+ Carnage Kabuto does not reflect DMG while affected by [Exit].
- Boss enemies in PvE cannot be affected by [Exit].
- Defeated units that are waiting to revive, such as Zombieman while downed, cannot be affected by [Exit].
- If only 1 enemy unit remains on the battlefield, that unit cannot be affected by [Exit].

### Additional Notes

- [Specialized Burn] is affected by HIT% and RES%.
- When transferring, [Specialized Burn] prioritizes characters that are not under any [Unyielding] state.
- [Specialized Burn] can stack up to 6 times.
- M. Choze and Vaccine Man can deal Extra DMG through [Specialized Burn].
- [Specialized Burn] can transfer to [Revived] and [Resurrected] characters.
- [Specialized Burn] is affected by Formal Set.
- [Specialized Burn] is not affected by [Evade].

The first time fatal DMG is taken each battle, revives with 50% of Max HP. |

**Effect Glossary:**

| Term | Description |
|---|---|
| [Heal Rate Reduction] | Reduces the effectiveness of HP recovery received by the affected unit. |
| [Agility] | Reduces higher Direct DMG by 12%–60% depending on the number of incoming strikes. Cannot coexist with [Non-Crit DMG Reduction]. |
| [Non-Crit DMG Reduction] | A defensive effect that reduces non-critical DMG and cannot coexist with [Agility]. |
